This setup queries poses relative to multiple past keyframes. An online pose-graph optimization system with loop closure suppresses long-range drift. Scal3R reaches convergence in 8 hours on a single GPU. It reduces the average ATE by over 60% on KITTI compared to the online baseline. It also achieves state-of-the-art performance across Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, and 7-Scenes. Project page: https://linjohnss.github.io/scal3r/

</details>


### [101] [TokenMatch: 3D Mesh Correspondence Transformer with Curvature-Guided Tokenisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04202)
*Adeela Islam,Zorah Lähner,Vittorio Murino,Vladislav Golyanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenMatch，一种基于Transformer的统一三维形状对应估计模型。它仅在BeCoS非等距部分-部分形状匹配数据集上训练，无需重训练即可推广到完整形状匹配。TokenMatch利用自注意力和交叉注意力机制学习patch级和点级关系及密集对应，核心创新在于通过曲率引导的自适应网格token化，实现高效且可解释的形状对应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的三维形状对应估计虽然在部分观测和非等距形变下仍具挑战性。现有学习方法依赖手工特征或模板表示，而基于函数映射的生成模型推理成本高、可解释性差，且对部分形状泛化能力不足。

Method: TokenMatch采用前馈Transformer架构，包含自注意力和交叉注意力模块以学习patch级和点级关系。核心思路是利用形状曲率引导将网格自适应token化为patches，学习形状特定的几何描述符，实现在部分-部分形状匹配上的训练并能零样本泛化到完整形状匹配。在BeCoS数据集上训练。

Result: 在CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE和SHREC'19等标准基准上评估，TokenMatch在部分与完整形状匹配的平均测地误差和交并比指标上大部分超越现有方法，且推理速度更快，亚秒级完成。

Conclusion: TokenMatch证明了基于曲率引导的自适应token化的Transformer模型能够在仅部分-部分形状数据上训练后有效泛化到完整形状匹配，实现了高效统一的三维形状对应估计，兼顾性能与速度。

Abstract: While data-driven 3D shape correspondence estimation has recently seen substantial progress, robust matching under partial observations and strong non-isometric deformations remains challenging. Existing learning-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted descriptors or template-based representations, whereas recent generative models over functional maps suffer from high inference cost, limited interpretability, and poor generalisation to partial shapes. In response to these limitations, this paper introduces TokenMatch, a new transformer-based unified model for estimating 3D shape correspondences. Our feed-forward approach trained exclusively on BeCoS, a challenging non-isometric partial-to-partial shape-matching dataset, can generalise to matching full shapes without retraining or fine-tuning. TokenMatch uses self- and cross-attention mechanisms to efficiently learn patch-level and point-level relations as well as dense correspondences between shape pairs. Our core insight is that meshes can be adaptively tokenised into patches using shape curvature guidance, enabling effective learning of shape-specific geometric descriptors for correspondence estimation. We evaluate TokenMatch on standard benchmarks for partial and full shape matching, including CP2P, PSMAL, BeCoS, FAUST, SCAPE, and SHREC'19. Our method achieves consistently high performance, in most cases outperforming existing methods for partial and full shape matching in the mean geodesic error and intersection-over-union metrics, while also running faster at sub-second inference speeds.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Learning Visual State Tracking in Videos Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04203)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Wenshuai Zhao,Mohammad Hassan Vali,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S³T是一个完全自包含的连续视频状态追踪框架，通过将时间采样密度视为特权信息实现自监督自蒸馏，无需标签、独立教师或奖励信号，在多个视频状态追踪基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续视频状态追踪方法通常依赖外部标签、独立教师模型或奖励信号，缺乏完全自包含的训练框架。作者提出假设：同一视频更密集的时间采样视图能够更准确地恢复运行状态，并据此设计无监督的自蒸馏方法。

Method: S³T将高密度采样的视频片段作为教师视图，稀疏采样的片段作为学生视图（共享相同权重），通过匹配稀疏视图的下一token分布来训练模型。模型自行生成目标，无需外部监督或额外推理成本。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S³T使VSTAT准确率提升+1.74（单模型）、+2.38（权重平均）、+2.70（额外视觉编码器适配）。从无标签合成视频学到的能力可迁移至真实视频：VSTAT-YouTube状态追踪问题提升+7.95，MVBench Action Count提升+4.50。

Conclusion: S³T是首个完全自包含的连续视频状态追踪框架，验证了时间采样密度作为特权信息的有效性。该自进化方法可显著提升状态追踪能力，且训练得到的技能能成功泛化到真实视频场景，优于以往在状态追踪上几乎无效的自进化方法。

Abstract: We introduce S$^3$T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for continuous video state tracking. Our method treats temporal sampling density as privileged information, based on the hypothesis that a denser view of the same clip recovers the running state more accurately. This view serves as the teacher, while a sparse-view student with the same weights learns to match its next-token distribution. The model generates its own target, so training requires no labels, separate teacher, or reward signal, and adds no inference cost. On LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, S$^3$T improves VSTAT accuracy by $+1.74$ as a single model, $+2.38$ with souping, and $+2.70$ with additional vision-encoder adaptation, while prior self-evolving methods leave state tracking largely unchanged. The capability learned from unlabeled synthetic clips transfers to real videos, improving performance by $+7.95$ on VSTAT-YouTube state-tracking questions and $+4.50$ on MVBench Action Count.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Learning from Scarce Labels: Multi-View Echocardiography for Ejection Fraction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02969)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Dominic Yurk,Yaser S. Abu-Mostafa*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文首次公开了用于从胸骨旁长轴（PLAX）超声心动图预测左心室射血分数（EF）的资源，通过创新的数据生成策略（结合临床记录与视频的时间相关性、视图分类器微调和代理标签）构建了超过25,000个PLAX视频的标注数据集，训练了首个可复现的PLAX EF模型（MAE 6.86%），并与A4C视图进行双重融合进一步降至6.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLAX视图在临床上常用于测量EF，但此前不存在公开的PLAX-EF数据集，且现有方法要么不可复现，要么依赖于难以获取的尖端四腔（A4C）视图。当心尖视图不可行时（如患者体位限制、肺部遮挡等），需要一种基于PLAX视图的可靠EF估计算法。

Method: 1) 提出时间相关性数据生成策略：利用临床记录和超声视频之间的时间关联实现自动标注；2) 微调视图分类器筛选并确认PLAX视图；3) 使用代理标法构建大规模标签数据集；4) 训练PLAX EF预测模型；5) 将PLAX与A4C预测通过简单的非加权晚期融合（late fusion）进行多视图整合。

Result: PLAX EF模型的MAE为6.86%，与临床标准的A4C方法（MAE 6%-7%）相当；PLAX与A4C的简单非加权晚期融合进一步将MAE降至6.37%，优于任一单视图基线。

Conclusion: 从PLAX视图进行EF估计在临床上是可行且具有相关性的，达到与A4C标准方法相当的精度，并在多视图融合下实现更优性能，为心尖视图不可行的场景提供了实用的临床解决方案。研究团队已公开数据集标签、模型和可运行演示以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first publicly available resource for predicting left ventricular ejection fraction (EF) from parasternal long-axis (PLAX) echocardiography. Because no PLAX-EF datasets previously existed, our work focuses on an innovative data generation strategy to overcome this scarcity. By leveraging a time-based correlation between clinical notes and echocardiographic videos, combined with fine-tuning view classifiers and proxy labeling, we created a labeled dataset of over 25,000 PLAX videos. This enables us to train the first reproducible PLAX EF model, achieving a mean absolute error (MAE) of 6.86%. Given that apical four-chamber (A4C) methods, the clinical standard, report MAE values of 6%-7%, our results demonstrate that EF estimation from PLAX views is both feasible and clinically relevant. This surpasses the performance of existing methods and provides a clinically relevant solution for situations where apical views may not be feasible. Going further, we demonstrate that combining PLAX and A4C predictions via simple unweighted late fusion improves both single-view baselines to a 6.37% MAE, underscoring the value of multi-view integration. To promote continued research, we release the dataset labels, trained models, and runnable demos on GitHub, Hugging Face, and Google Colab: https://github.com/Jeffrey4899/PLAX_EF_Labels_202509

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Blur: A Semantic Tri-view Pipeline for Teledermatology Gradability via Skin Micro-relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03095)
*Robert Engel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义三视图流水线（Semantic Tri-view Pipeline），一种可解释的远程皮肤病学可分级性筛查架构，通过将表皮微纹理形式化为可计算的图像质量生物标志物，利用轻量级DeepLabV3+模型分割微纹理保真度，并跨多个照片视角聚合以提升鲁棒性。在SCIN数据集上，从多数共识样本的AUC 0.81（PPV 80.6%）提升到光学无歧义一致样本的AUC 0.96（PPV 97.7%），实现了隐私友好、边缘可部署的实时病例提交反馈系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动远程皮肤病学工作流程中，评估提交病例照片的可诊断性（gradability）是关键的瓶颈步骤。皮肤科医生通常需查看多个视角（局部、成角和特写）的照片来识别一致的纹理细节，而非依赖单一图像。现有方法缺乏可解释且能利用多视角冗余的自动化筛选机制。

Method: 提出语义三视图流水线架构：(1) 使用专家标注的SCIN公共数据集子集，训练轻量级DeepLabV3+模型分割表皮微纹理保真度；(2) 将空间掩膜跨至多三个病例视图聚合，通过逻辑回归分类器利用视角冗余增强在非受控智能手机采集条件下的鲁棒性；(3) 学习上下文感知且临床可解释的启发式规则，如对局部远视图中高保真纹理进行惩罚。

Result: 在预设90%灵敏度的操作点上，系统表现良好，其表观错误主要反映主观临床变异（如临床医生依赖非视觉元数据的临界案例）。在SCIN上，性能从方差较大的多数共识案例（AUC 0.81，PPV 80.6%）提升到光学无歧义的一致案例（AUC 0.96，PPV 97.7%）。

Conclusion: 该系统是一个可解释、隐私设计优先、边缘就绪的远程皮肤病学分级系统，能够在病例提交期间提供实时反馈，在人工审查前过滤掉不可分级的照片集。

Abstract: Smartphone skin photographs are indispensable to teledermatology, yet assessing the diagnostic suitability of submitted cases (gradability) remains a critical bottleneck in mobile care workflows. Dermatologists routinely review multiple photographic views (regional, angled, and close-up) to identify consistent textural detail rather than relying on a single image. We present the Semantic Tri-view Pipeline, an interpretable architecture for automated teledermatology gradability screening that formalizes epidermal micro-relief as a computable biomarker of image quality. Using an expert-annotated subset of the public SCIN dataset, we train a lightweight DeepLabV3+ model to segment micro-relief fidelity. These spatial masks are then aggregated across up to three case views with a logistic regression classifier, leveraging viewpoint redundancy to support robustness under uncontrolled smartphone acquisition. This approach learns context-aware, clinically intelligible heuristics, such as penalizing high-fidelity texture in regional distance views. Evaluated at a predefined 90% sensitivity operating point, the system's apparent errors largely reflect subjective clinical variance on borderline cases where clinicians rely on non-visual metadata. On SCIN, performance improves from an AUC of 0.81 (80.6% PPV) on variance-heavy majority-consensus cases to 0.96 (97.7% PPV) on optically unambiguous unanimous cases. Overall, this work delivers an interpretable, privacy-by-design, edge-ready system that can provide real-time feedback during case submission to filter ungradable photo sets before review.

</details>


### [105] [Plant Growth Estimation with a Camera-Based Vegetation Index Mapping System for Agricultural Ground Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03872)
*Lukas Pindl,Michael Maier,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于摄像头的植物生长测绘传感器，利用RTK-GNSS将多光谱图像融合到田间地图上，通过NDVI指数评估植物生长，采用透视投影到地面平面的方法实现低端硬件上的实时处理，并与商用传感器对比验证了趋势一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用车辆搭载的植物生长传感器成本高、绝对数值难以直接比较，且无法区分地面与非地面区域，作物密度会对平均NDVI测量产生干扰。因此需要一种低成本、实时、可区分地物类型的替代方案。

Method: 采用RTK-GNSS辅助的多光谱相机成像系统，将相机图像通过透视投影映射到估算的地面平面上，利用NDVI作为植物生长指数（可根据相机更换其他指数），实现图像拼接与地面/非地面区域的滤波处理。

Result: 与商用车载传感器对比显示，虽然绝对数值难以直接比较，但两种传感器呈现相似的变化趋势；该方案支持地面与非地面区域过滤，可降低作物密度对平均NDVI的影响，且计算复杂度低，可在低端硬件上实时处理。

Conclusion: 该摄像头方案可作为商用传感器的可行替代，具备实时处理、低成本硬件的优势，并能通过地面滤波提高NDVI测量的准确性，未来可适配不同相机和指数扩展应用场景。

Abstract: This work presents a camera-based sensor for mapping plant growth over an agricultural field. The sensor can be used on ground-based vehicles like a tractor and relies on RTK-GNSS to correctly merge many multispectral images onto one map. NDVI is used as an index to estimate plant growth, but the approach can be used with other indices as well depending on the camera. The images from the camera are projected onto the estimated ground plane using perspective projection. This computationally simple approach allows for real-time processing of all images on the field even with low-end hardware. The results are compared to a commercially established vehicle-mounted sensor. Absolute values are hard to compare, but both sensors show similar trends. The camera-based approach also allows for filtering of ground and non-ground areas, potentially reducing the impact of crop density on the average measured NDVI.

</details>


### [106] [Computationally Efficient Pathology Segmentation using Knowledge Distillation from Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03947)
*Jiaqi Lv,Yijie Zhu,Saki Okada,Maxwell Stanley Renna,Robert Goldin,Stefan Antonowicz,Shan E Ahmed Raza*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于基础模型知识蒸馏的框架，用于高效的组织分割。通过将Virchow2基础模型的知识转移到紧凑的学生网络中，在保持高性能的同时显著减少参数量并提升推理吞吐量（最高达10倍），在四个数据集上达到最优或接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织切片全切片图像（WSIs）的大规模分析需要自动组织分割，但面临三个核心挑战：(1) 像素级标注成本高昂；(2) 自然图像预训练模型在病理学上迁移效果差；(3) 病理基础模型虽然表征能力强，但计算开销大，难以大规模部署。因此需要一种既能利用基础模型强大表示能力又保持高效推理的解决方案。

Method: 提出基础模型知识蒸馏框架，分两步：首先训练基于Virchow2的分割教师网络（包括LoRA适配变体），在四个数据集上取得最优或接近最优性能；然后将教师网络的响应级（response-level）和特征级（feature-level）知识蒸馏到紧凑的学生网络中。

Result: 知识蒸馏一致性地优于仅监督训练的学生模型；学生模型在各数据集上达到最优或接近最优的性能，同时参数量显著减少，推理吞吐量相比基于基础模型的分割网络提升最高达10倍。在PUMA、IGNITE、BEETLE以及私有血管分割数据集上均验证有效。公开数据集上的模型将通过TIAToolbox发布。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基础模型的知识可以有效地转移到高效的分割模型中，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现可扩展的大规模部署，解决了病理基础模型计算开销大的实际应用瓶颈。

Abstract: Automatic tissue segmentation is essential for large-scale analysis of histopathology whole-slide images (WSIs), but accurate pixel-level segmentation remains challenging. Pixel-level annotations are expensive to obtain, models pre-trained on natural images may transfer poorly to histopathology, and pathology foundation models, despite their strong representations, are computationally expensive to deploy at scale. We address these challenges with a foundation-model knowledge distillation framework for efficient tissue segmentation. We first train Virchow2-based segmentation teachers, including a LoRA-adapted variant, achieving state-of-the-art or highly competitive performance across four datasets: PUMA, IGNITE, BEETLE, and a private blood vessel segmentation dataset. We then transfer response-level and feature-level knowledge from these teachers into compact student networks. Distillation consistently improves student performance over supervised training alone, producing state-of-the-art or near state-of-the-art results with substantially fewer parameters and up to ten-fold higher inference throughput than foundation-model-based segmentation networks. These results show that foundation-model knowledge can be effectively transferred to efficient segmentation models for scalable deployment without compromising performance. Models trained on the public datasets will be released through TIAToolbox.

</details>
